## ADR-025-AR — 4 exported webview catalogs/tables + the `EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS` shared singleton made deeply immutable (task F11-1, WS-F11, SY-02)

**Outcome (task F11-1, WS-F11 — 1 task, 1 commit):** re-grounded by symbol on `ae23f24` (F7-3b-closed HEAD, the live tip at kickoff) — the finding's 4 sites matched exactly: `MENTIONS` (`mentionCatalog.ts:16`), `SLASH_TEMPLATES` (`slashCatalog.ts:32`), `STATUS` + `UNKNOWN_MCP_STATUS` (`McpPanel.tsx:36`/`:45`), `EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS` (`setupCards.ts:218`) — all plain mutable `T[]` / `Record<...>` / `{}` literals, every current consumer read-only (BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING, zero test edits).

**House idiom, grounded twice, split by shape.** Arrays of objects: `ignoreFilter.ts`'s `DEFAULT_IGNORE_PATTERNS: readonly string[]` and `nextEditCopy.ts`'s `NEXT_EDIT_ROWS: ReadonlyArray<NextEditRowCopy>` — a `readonly`-typed literal, NO runtime `Object.freeze`, for a **latent-only** catalog (no consumer mutates today). Records/singletons carrying a real shared-instance hazard: `DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS` (`src/autocomplete/endpoints.ts`), `GRAMMAR_FILE_BY_LANGUAGE` (`WebTreeSitterParser.ts`), `DIFF_LINE_PREFIX` (`codeActionSerialize.ts`) — `Readonly<Record<K,V>>` + `Object.freeze`, and `ZERO_RANGE` (`src/mcp/lsp/tools.ts`) additionally nests `Object.freeze` per object-valued entry for genuine deep freeze. F11-1 matched EACH exemplar to its own shape rather than forcing one idiom onto all four sites.

**Arrays — `MENTIONS`/`SLASH_TEMPLATES` (compile-time only, matching `ignoreFilter`/`nextEditCopy`).** `export const MENTIONS: readonly Readonly<MentionItem>[] = [ … ];` and the parallel `SLASH_TEMPLATES: readonly Readonly<SlashTemplate>[]`. The outer `readonly … []` blocks `.push`/index-write on the catalog; wrapping each element in `Readonly<>` additionally blocks a per-entry field write (`MENTIONS[0].label = …`) — the review-focus (a) requirement that immutability "reach the elements", which the named exemplars alone do not (`NextEditRowCopy`'s own fields aren't marked `readonly`). Proven via an isolated `tsc` scratch check (3 variants) BEFORE touching the real files: an explicit wide `readonly MentionItem[] = [...] as const` annotation was tried first and found NOT to block `MENTIONS[0].label = 'x'` — the explicit wide annotation overrides `as const`'s literal-narrowing, so that combo (the brief's first-suggested form) does not actually reach the elements. `readonly Readonly<T>[]` (no `as const` needed) does: confirmed by a `@ts-expect-error`-gated scratch file where both `.push(...)` and `[0].field = …` produced real tsc errors, and reads back into every real consumer (`filterMentions(): MentionItem[]`, `TEMPLATE_IDS = new Set(SLASH_TEMPLATES.map(...))`, `buildSlashSections`'s `items: readonly (SlashTemplate|AgentSlashItem)[]`) with zero cast — an object typed with `readonly` fields is still structurally assignable to the same shape without them (TS readonly-modifier compatibility is not part of an object type's identity outside arrays/tuples), confirmed by dedicated scratch checks before editing the real files.

**Records — `STATUS`/`UNKNOWN_MCP_STATUS` (`Readonly<Record<…>>` + nested `Object.freeze`, matching `DEFAULT_ENDPOINTS`/`ZERO_RANGE`).** `STATUS: Readonly<Record<McpStatus, Readonly<{tone; label; icon}>>>` = `Object.freeze({ connected: Object.freeze({...}), disconnected: Object.freeze({...}) })`; `UNKNOWN_MCP_STATUS` gets the single-object form of the same pattern. Unlike the two catalogs above, these get BOTH the readonly type AND a runtime `Object.freeze` — this repo's dominant `src/` idiom for a `Record`-shaped static lookup table (7+ exemplars grounded), and belt-and-suspenders cheap here since both are tiny 2-entry/1-entry literals. `totalLookup<K,V>(map: Record<K,V>, key, fallback: V)` (`webview/src/lookup.ts`) still accepts the `Readonly`-typed `STATUS`/`UNKNOWN_MCP_STATUS` unchanged, confirmed by a scratch reproduction of the exact `totalLookup(STATUS, srv.status, UNKNOWN_MCP_STATUS)` call site plus `@ts-expect-error`-gated mutation attempts (`STATUS.connected = …`, `STATUS.connected.label = …`, `UNKNOWN_MCP_STATUS.label = …`) all correctly rejected.

**The singleton — `EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS` (RUNTIME-frozen, review focus (b)).** `export const EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS: Readonly<SetupProgressMap> = Object.freeze({});` — this is the ONE genuinely shared-instance hazard the finding names: every fresh `AppState.setupProgress` (`types.ts`'s `createInitialState`) starts as this SAME object reference, so a stray direct write anywhere would corrupt the shared default for every tab that hasn't yet received its own `setup.progress` push. A compile-time-only `readonly` annotation is insurance against `tsc`-checked call sites only; `Object.freeze` additionally makes the guard hold at runtime (strict-mode ESM throws `TypeError` on a blocked write), matching this repo's `Object.freeze`-guarded-singleton idiom (`EMPTY_SNAPSHOT`, `src/autocomplete/context/contextService.ts`).

**`foldSetupProgress` re-grounded — confirmed ALREADY copy-first, no fix needed (review focus (c)).** Read line-by-line: the `done===true` branch either returns `map` UNCHANGED (same reference, no-op) or builds `next = { ...map }` then `delete next[key]` before returning `next`; the update branch returns `{ ...map, [key]: entry }`. Every exit either returns the SAME reference untouched or a NEW object built by spread — `map[key] = …` direct-write is nowhere in the function. This is the pre-existing, correct posture the finding hoped for; freezing the empty `{}` singleton cannot break it, confirmed by running `setupCards.test.ts`'s `foldSetupProgress` suite (both the accumulation cases and the `§7.2.2` done-marker deletion cases, all seeded from `EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS`) unedited and green.

**Mutation-guard proof (review focus, brief step 4) — both tsc AND runtime.** In a temporary scratch append (reverted before commit, confirmed by `grep`+re-typecheck): `EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS.foo = 1;` → **TS2322** (`number` not assignable to `SetupProgressEntry`) + **TS2542** (`Index signature in type 'Readonly<SetupProgressMap>' only permits reading`); `MENTIONS.push({...})` → **TS2339** (`Property 'push' does not exist on type 'readonly Readonly<MentionItem>[]'`). Both reverted byte-identical (`git diff --stat` before/after the scratch round-trip shows only the intended 4-file diff); `tsc` re-confirmed 0 errors post-revert. Runtime-freeze half of the guard (strict-mode `TypeError` on a blocked write to the frozen `EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS`) follows directly from `Object.freeze` semantics in an ES module (this bundle's `verbatimModuleSyntax`/`module: ESNext` output) — not re-demonstrated by a throwaway runtime script, since the tsc-level rejection above already makes the write unreachable through any type-checked call site, and `foldSetupProgress`'s confirmed copy-first posture means production code never attempts one.

**Consumers read-only + compile + suites 0-EDIT green (review focus (d)).** `filterMentions` (`mentionCatalog.ts`) / `mentionChip.ts`'s `ICON_BY_KIND`/`LABEL_BY_KIND` maps; `SLASH_TEMPLATES.map/.filter`/`for…of` in `slashCatalog.ts` + `slashCatalog.test.ts`; `STATUS[...]`/`totalLookup` reads in `McpPanel.tsx` + `McpPanel.test.ts`; `foldSetupProgress(EMPTY_SETUP_PROGRESS, …)` in `setupCards.ts` + `setupCards.test.ts` + `types.ts`'s `createInitialState` — all re-ground and confirmed READ-ONLY, zero writes. `npx vitest run webview/`: 87 files, 1821 passed, 0 edits to any test file.

**No type-hole `as`-cast (review focus (f)).** `as const` was tried and DROPPED (see the arrays analysis above — it doesn't survive an explicit wide annotation, so it earned no place in the final diff); the final diff contains zero `as`-casts of any kind (sanctioned or otherwise), zero `any`, zero new `!`, zero `{ …x, }` spread. Confirmed by a `git diff -U0` scan of every added line.

**Count discipline.** Baseline (`ae23f24`, F7-3b-closed HEAD) full `npm run gate`: **350 test files, 7662 passed | 15 skipped (7677), tsc 0 (host + webview)**. After F11-1 (4-file production diff, zero test edits): **350 files, 7662 passed | 15 skipped (7677), tsc 0** — identical count, the expected signature of a behaviour-preserving type-safety fix. One full-gate run mid-task hit the known `src/rag/indexer.test.ts` Windows timer-drain flake (`drainUntil` timeout in an unrelated RAG watch test, not an assertion failure) — isolate-running `src/rag/indexer.test.ts` alone immediately after confirmed 59 passed | 2 skipped (61), and the very next full-gate re-run came back clean at **350|7662|15(7677)** with zero failures (this entry's final state).

## ADR-025-AS — `requestShaped`/`requestShapedOptional` fold the invoke+requireShape RPC-wrapper boilerplate ×11 (task F1-1, WS-F1 kickoff, FI-35)

**Outcome (task F1-1, WS-F1 — kickoff task, 1 commit):** re-grounded by symbol on `1991d04` (F11-1-closed HEAD, the live tip at kickoff) — F11-1 touched `webview/composer`/`webview/panels`, not `App.tsx`, so every line number the brief cited (`requireShape` at `:116`, the 8 always-shape sites, the 3 undefined-passthrough checkpoint sites) matched exactly. `requireShape<T>(raw, guard, method): T` (`App.tsx:116`) already existed; the repeated invoke+shape tail — `await bridge.request(method, params[, tag])` then `requireShape(result, guard, method)`, with `method` written TWICE per site (a drift risk) — is now folded into two module-level helpers adjacent to `requireShape`, and the 11 call sites become one-line (or short-block) callers.

**`bridge` scope + `bridge.request`/`RpcClient.request` tag semantics, grounded FIRST (review focus (b)).** `bridge` is `bridge.ts`'s own top-level singleton (`export const bridge = new Bridge();`), imported into `App.tsx` at MODULE scope (`import { bridge } from './bridge'`) — not component-scoped, exactly like `requireShape` itself. The two new helpers are therefore plain module-level `async function`s beside `requireShape`, closing over nothing component-specific — **no new module global was introduced**; `bridge` was already one. `Bridge.request(method, params?, tag?)` forwards straight to `RpcClient.request`, which builds the pending-request entry as `{ resolve, reject, clearTimer, ...(tag !== undefined ? { tag } : {}) }` (`rpc.ts:169-174`) — `tag` is spread onto the entry ONLY when `!== undefined`. Passing `tag === undefined` therefore behaves IDENTICALLY to omitting the argument entirely (both produce a `tag`-less pending entry, i.e. an untagged/connection-global request never reachable by `rejectByTag`). Confirmed by reading `rpc.ts` line-by-line before writing either helper — **no branch on `tag === undefined` was needed**; both helpers accept a single optional 4th `tag?: string` parameter and pass it straight through.

**The two helpers.** Both typed `method: ControlRequestMethod` (imported fresh into `App.tsx`'s existing type-only `./protocol` import list — all 11 real call sites' literal methods are members of `CONTROL_METHODS`, so this is the exact, non-widened type `bridge.request`'s own first parameter already demands — no `as`-cast needed to satisfy it):
```
async function requestShaped<T>(method, params, guard, tag?): Promise<T> {
  const result = await bridge.request(method, params, tag);
  return requireShape(result, guard, method);
}
async function requestShapedOptional<T>(method, params, guard, tag?): Promise<T | undefined> {
  const result = await bridge.request(method, params, tag);
  return result === undefined ? undefined : requireShape(result, guard, method);
}
```
`method` is now passed to each helper ONCE — used for both the `bridge.request` call and the `requireShape` error label — single-sourcing what every one of the 11 sites previously wrote as a doubled literal.

**11 handlers folded, split exactly as the brief predicted (review focus (a)/(c)/(d)).** **8 always-shape, UNTAGGED, via `requestShaped`:** `addMcpServer`→`mcp.add`, `testMcpServer`→`mcp.test`, `authMcpServer`→`mcp.auth`, `mcpCatalog`→`mcp.catalog`, `mcpCatalogInstall`→`mcp.catalogInstall`, `previewHubSkill`→`skills.hubPreview`, `scanHubSkill`→`skills.hubScan`, `installHubSkill`→`skills.hubInstall`. **3 undefined-passthrough, TAGGED `tab.tabId`, via `requestShapedOptional`:** `restoreCheckpoint`→`checkpoint.restore`, `redoCheckpoint`→`checkpoint.redo`, `redoAllCheckpoint`→`checkpoint.redoAll`. Every handler's own per-call PARAM CONSTRUCTION stays INLINE, unfolded: the `if (force) params.force = true` conditional in all 3 checkpoint handlers; the `interface`-vs-`type` `wireParams` rebuild in `mcpCatalogInstall` (`McpCatalogInstallParams` is an `interface` — TS infers no implicit index signature for it, so it cannot pass directly as `Record<string, unknown>` the way the `type`-alias `McpAddParams` can; this rebuild predates F1-1 and is untouched). `createSkill` carries the identical `wireParams` rebuild pattern but is NOT part of this fold — it returns `bridge.request(...)` raw (no shaped result), so it (along with `removeMcpServer`/`setMcpServerEnabled`/`reloadMcp`/`uninstallHubSkill`/`toggle`/`setConfig`/`setNextEditToggle`) is confirmed BYTE-UNTOUCHED in the diff.

**Characterization test added BEFORE the fold (review focus (f), TDD).** Grepped every existing suite for an App-level (not panel-level) exercise of the checkpoint undefined-passthrough: `App.dom.test.tsx`'s existing CF-12 redo describe block only ever mocks `bridge.request` to resolve a REAL `CheckpointRestoreResult`, never `undefined`; `CheckpointsPanel.dom.test.tsx`'s T-C2/IMP-4 tests DO cover `undefined`, but only against an injected `onRestore`/`onRedo` test double, never the real `App.tsx`-owned `restoreCheckpoint` this fold touches. Zero existing test exercised the fold's actual mutation surface end-to-end. Added one new characterization test to `App.dom.test.tsx` (real `<App>`, real `bridge.request` spy resolving `undefined` for `checkpoint.restore` only): bind a tab to a root, navigate to Checkpoints, push a `panel.data` checkpoint row, click Restore → confirm "Restore workspace", assert `CheckpointsPanel`'s own T-C2 copy — "Restore failed — the host returned no result" — renders (the panel's independent defense-in-depth branch that a raw shape-guard throw would NOT produce). Confirmed **PASS pre-fold** (isolated run, 1 passed), confirmed it stays **PASS, 0-edit, post-fold**.

**Mutation-guard proof (review focus, brief step 4).** Temporarily dropped the `result === undefined ? undefined :` passthrough inside `requestShapedOptional` (always routing through `requireShape`) — the new characterization test FLIPPED to a failure (it hung on `findByText(/Restore failed — the host returned no result/i)` because the mutated helper instead threw `"checkpoint.restore returned an unrecognized result shape"`, an unhandled rejection the panel's `restore()` `.then(...,(err)=>...)` branch turns into a DIFFERENT message, never the T-C2 copy the assertion looks for). Reverted the mutation; re-ran `npx vitest run webview/` — 87 files, 1822 passed, back to green.

**Count discipline.** Baseline (`1991d04`, F11-1-closed HEAD, per `ADR-025-AR`'s own after-count) full `npm run gate`: **350 test files, 7662 passed | 15 skipped (7677), tsc 0 (host + webview)**. Full-gate run after this task: **350 test files (350), 7663 passed | 15 skipped (7678), tsc 0 (host + webview), no type errors.** Delta: **+1 test** (exactly the new checkpoint-undefined-passthrough characterization test), **0 new test files** (added to the existing `App.dom.test.tsx`), tsc 0. No indexer flake on this run.

**Byte-scan (2 touched files):** `webview/src/App.tsx` — NUL: 0, U+2028: 0, U+2029: 0. `webview/src/App.dom.test.tsx` — NUL: 0, U+2028: 0, U+2029: 0.

**No `any`/new `!`/spread/`as`-cast (review focus (f)).** `git diff -U0` scan of every added line across both files: zero `any` (types or tests), zero `catch (e: any)`, zero new non-null `!` assertions, zero `{ …x, }` trailing-comma spreads, zero `as`-casts (the one regex hit was prose — "surfacing as a DIFFERENT failure" inside a doc comment, not a cast).

**App.tsx line count: 1396 → 1418 (net +22), reported honestly.** `git diff --numstat`: 65 insertions / 43 deletions in `App.tsx`. The 11 call sites themselves shrank by roughly 42 lines net (each 3-6 line invoke-then-shape block collapsed to 1-3 lines), but the two new helpers carry ~45 lines of doc comment (this repo's established density for a shared cross-cutting seam — matching `requireShape`'s own doc immediately above them) — so THIS task's own file grows slightly even though it deletes ×11 duplicated tails. The win is not this commit's line count; it is that every FUTURE WS-F1 task touching a correlated-RPC handler (feeding FI-04's broader `App.tsx` decomposition) now calls a one-line helper instead of repaying the 3-6 line invoke+shape tail — the shrink compounds forward, it does not land in this single diff.

**★ FI-35 closed.** `requestShaped`/`requestShapedOptional` single-source the invoke+shape tail for all 11 correlated RPC handlers that shape a result — 8 untagged always-shape sites and 3 `tab.tabId`-tagged undefined-passthrough checkpoint sites — with `method` passed once (removing the doubled-literal drift risk), every handler's own per-call param construction left inline, and the 8 non-shaping handlers confirmed byte-untouched. ## ADR-025-AT — `RecoveryRow` extraction of the 3 duplicated status/recovery rows (task F1-2, WS-F1, FI-36)

**Outcome (task F1-2, WS-F1 — second task, 1 commit, BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING, NOT `[SEC]`/`[CONC]`):** re-grounded by symbol on `7e66f0b` (F1-1-closed HEAD, the live tip at kickoff) — F1-1 folded the RPC-wrapper boilerplate at `App.tsx:~505-620`, which shifted the 3 recovery rows down from the brief's `1991d04` line numbers to `:995-1043`; every className string, `Icon` prop, button shape, and gate predicate matched the brief's grounding byte-for-byte once re-read at the live tip. Extracted `webview/src/components/RecoveryRow.tsx` — a pure presentational component (icon + message + optional action button) — and rewrote the 3 sibling rows (openFailed/Reconnect "G-9", sessionLost/History "ARCH-1", newSessionPending "UX-04a") as `RecoveryRow` callers, each keeping its own DISTINCT visibility gate inline verbatim (they are different `tab` predicates, not the component's concern). The trailing permanently-mounted `<LiveRegion text={tab.newSessionPending === true ? 'Starting a new session…' : ''} className="sr-only" />` immediately after the third row is untouched and stays OUTSIDE `RecoveryRow` (Finding-7 discipline: the region itself is never conditionally mounted).

**Prop shape decision — `icon.className` made REQUIRED, not optional (review focus (d)).** The brief's illustrative signature proposed `icon: { name; spin?; className? }` with a conditional-spread adapter to forward `spin`/`className` into `Icon`'s own props, then asked to avoid that banned `{ ...x }` pattern in favour of "explicit optional props… if Icon already accepts optional spin/className, pass them directly." Grounding `Icon.tsx` first: `spin?: boolean | undefined` is EXPLICITLY typed with the `| undefined` union (so any `boolean | undefined` value forwards into it cleanly under `exactOptionalPropertyTypes`, `webview/tsconfig.json:19`), but `className?: string` is NOT — it carries the implicit optional form only. Forwarding a `string | undefined` value into that implicit-optional slot is exactly the class of error `App.tsx` itself already documents twice at the two existing exactOptionalPropertyTypes comments (the `hiddenCount ?? 0` note and the `loadNotice`/spread-omission note a few hundred lines below); `ConfirmStrip.tsx`'s own `className?: string | undefined` prop (declared with the explicit union specifically so IT can be forwarded) confirms the codebase's established fix for THIS direction is either the explicit-union declaration or a coalesce, never a straight pass. Since all 3 real call sites always supply a `className` (verified by re-reading each: `"flex-none text-warn"` ×2, `"flex-none"` ×1 — none ever omit it), the honest fix was to make `RecoveryRow`'s own `icon.className` field **required** (`className: string`, not `className?: string`) rather than declare an optionality no caller exercises. This sidesteps the exactOptionalPropertyTypes hazard entirely — a required `string` is always assignable into `Icon`'s optional `className?: string` — with zero coercion and zero banned spread. `icon.spin` stays optional (`spin?: boolean | undefined`, only the newSessionPending row supplies it) and forwards straight through (`spin={icon.spin}`) since `Icon.spin`'s own declared type already includes `| undefined`; the other two rows omit the `spin` key entirely (key omission, never `= undefined`, matching the codebase-wide convention documented at `src/mcp/search.ts:14`/`src/host/checkpoints/trackerRegistry.ts:122`/etc.). `action` is a plain `action?: { label: string; onClick: () => void }`, checked via `!== undefined` inside the component — no forwarding, no exactOptionalPropertyTypes exposure — and the newSessionPending row omits the key entirely (no button).

**DOM byte-identity (review focus (a)).** `RecoveryRow`'s render is `<div className="flex items-center gap-2 border-b border-border bg-surface px-3 py-2 text-2xs text-muted"><Icon name={icon.name} size={12} spin={icon.spin} className={icon.className} /><span className="min-w-0 flex-1">{message}</span>{action !== undefined && <button type="button" onClick={action.onClick} className="flex-none rounded border border-border px-1.5 py-0.5 text-2xs text-fg hover:bg-overlay">{action.label}</button>}</div>` — the div/icon/span/button className strings and `size={12}` are copied verbatim from the 3 original rows (confirmed by diff: every deleted line's class string reappears unchanged either in `RecoveryRow.tsx` or in the caller's `icon`/`action` object literal). `git diff -U0` of `App.tsx` shows exactly the 3 row bodies replaced by `RecoveryRow` calls plus the `Icon` import removal (now unused in `App.tsx` — its sole other appearance was prose inside a doc comment, not JSX) and the new `RecoveryRow` import; no other line in the file changed.

**3 gates preserved inline verbatim (review focus (b)):** `!tab.error && tab.openFailed === true && tab.binding !== 'bound' && tab.newSessionPending !== true`; `!tab.error && tab.sessionLost === true && tab.binding !== 'bound' && tab.newSessionPending !== true`; `tab.newSessionPending === true && !tab.error` — none merged, reordered, or altered; each stays the `{gate && <RecoveryRow … />}` shape with its own comment block (G-9/ARCH-1/UX-04a doc comments) left exactly in place above it.

**Row-3 correctly omits the button; `sessionLostRowCopy`/`bridge.post`/`dispatch` preserved per row (review focus (c)/(e)).** Row 1's `action.onClick` is `() => bridge.post({ type: 'tab.open', tabId: tab.tabId })` (unchanged). Row 2's `message` is still `sessionLostRowCopy(tab.sessionLostReason)` (the helper call site, not its body, is untouched) and its `action.onClick` is still `() => dispatch({ local: { type: 'local.setPanel', panel: 'sessions' } })`. Row 3 passes no `action` prop at all — `RecoveryRow` renders no button — and its `icon` carries `spin: true`.

**TDD (review focus (f)).** Wrote `webview/src/components/RecoveryRow.dom.test.tsx` FIRST — 3 tests: (1) full chrome + action button, asserting the div's 10 exact classes, the icon's `.codicon-warning` class + `flex-none text-warn` + `fontSize: 12px` style + absence of `codicon-modifier-spin`, and the button's `type="button"` + 9 exact classes; (2) the spin-and-no-action shape (`.codicon-loading` + `codicon-modifier-spin flex-none`, `queryByRole('button')` absent); (3) the action `onClick` fires exactly once on click. Ran it against the not-yet-created component first — RED: `Failed to resolve import "./RecoveryRow"` (fails because the feature is missing, not a typo). Wrote the minimal `RecoveryRow.tsx` — GREEN: 3/3 passed. Rewired the 3 `App.tsx` rows as callers; `npx vitest run webview/` — 88 files, 1825 passed (was 87/1822 pre-task), 0 edits to `App.dom.test.tsx` or any other existing test file (confirmed by `git status`/`git diff --stat`: only `App.tsx` modified, both `RecoveryRow.*` files new).

**Mutation-guard proof (review focus (f), brief step 4).** Dropped `border-b` from `RecoveryRow.tsx`'s div className (`"flex items-center gap-2 border-border bg-surface …"`) — re-ran the new test file: 1 of 3 FAILED (`toHaveClass` mismatch, the exact expected-vs-received classlist printed). Reverted the mutation; re-ran — 3/3 passed again. The chrome is provably load-bearing, not merely asserted.

**Count discipline.** Baseline (`7e66f0b`, F1-1-closed HEAD, per `ADR-025-AS`'s own after-count) full `npm run gate`: **350 test files, 7663 passed | 15 skipped (7678), tsc 0 (host + webview).** Full-gate run after this task: **351 test files (+1, the new `RecoveryRow.dom.test.tsx`), 7666 passed | 15 skipped (7681), tsc 0 (host + webview), no type errors.** Delta: **+1 test file, +3 tests**, tsc 0. No `src/rag/indexer.test.ts` Windows-teardown flake on this run.

The chrome itself (div/icon/span/button + doc comments) now lives once, in `RecoveryRow.tsx` (52 lines including its house-style doc header), instead of three times inline.

## ADR-025-AU — `PanelScaffold` single-sources the 9 panel-render scaffolds' role/aria/class chrome (task F1-3, WS-F1, FI-14)

**Outcome (task F1-3, WS-F1 — third task, 1 commit, BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING + a11y-preserving, NOT `[SEC]`/`[CONC]`):** re-grounded by symbol on `2c57b3e` (F1-2-closed HEAD, the live tip at kickoff) — F1-2's `RecoveryRow` extraction shifted the 9 panel scaffolds down from the brief's `7e66f0b` line numbers to `App.tsx:1147-1403`; re-reading each of the 9 (`tools`/`mcp`/`skills`/`checkpoints`/`subagents`/`sessions`/`models`/`setup`/`settings`) confirmed **zero drift** — every one shared the exact `<div id={panelTabpanelId(key)} role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby={panelTabDomId(key)} className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col"><ErrorBoundary region="the X panel">…</ErrorBoundary></div>` shape the brief described, so no override prop or inline-left exception was needed. Extracted `webview/src/components/PanelScaffold.tsx` — a pure structural wrapper (`panel`/`region`/`children` props) — and rewrote all 9 scaffolds as `{state.activePanel === '<key>' && (<PanelScaffold panel="<key>" region="<region>"><XxxPanel …/></PanelScaffold>)}`, keeping each `state.activePanel === '<key>'` gate and the inner `<XxxPanel …/>` (with its full prop list, including `sessions`'s exactOptional spread-omission props and `checkpoints`'s scoped-refresh-error prop) completely unchanged. The `chat` panel (`App.tsx:~1035`, its own outer wrapper + a nested B1 `role="log"` scroll region + `chatPanelMounted` logic) is structurally different and was correctly left inline, untouched.

**`PanelKey` type — grounded as the existing `Panel` union, no new type/no cast (review focus (e)).** The brief asked to "ground the exact `PanelKey` type App uses for these keys" so `panelTabpanelId`/`panelTabDomId` accept `props.panel` without a cast; grounding found no separate `PanelKey` — both helpers (`components/PriorityTabs.tsx:35,49`) are already typed `(panel: Panel): string`, where `Panel` (`src/shared/protocol.ts:113-123`, re-exported through `webview/src/protocol.ts`'s `export type *`) is the 10-member union `'chat' | 'tools' | 'mcp' | 'skills' | 'checkpoints' | 'subagents' | 'sessions' | 'models' | 'settings' | 'setup'` — the same type `App.tsx` itself imports for this purpose. `PanelScaffold`'s `panel` prop is declared `panel: Panel` (imported `import type { Panel } from '../protocol';`, the identical specifier/convention `PriorityTabs.tsx` and `OverflowMenu.tsx` already use from `components/`), so each of the 9 string-literal call sites (`panel="tools"`, `panel="mcp"`, …) narrows structurally and flows into both helpers with zero cast. `Panel` being a superset that also includes `'chat'` is harmless — `PanelScaffold` is simply never called with `'chat'` in production, matching the brief's intent without needing a narrower excluding type.

**`region` passed explicitly, never derived (review focus (b)).** `PanelScaffold` takes `region: string` as a caller-supplied prop and forwards it straight to `<ErrorBoundary region={region}>` — there is no capitalize-the-key helper anywhere in the component. Each of the 9 call sites spells its region literal out by hand, copied verbatim from the original inline `ErrorBoundary region="…"` strings: `"the Tools panel"`, **`"the MCP panel"`** (uppercase — the case the brief specifically flagged as unrecoverable from a naive `panel[0].toUpperCase() + panel.slice(1)` capitalize, since `'mcp'` would derive to `"Mcp panel"` not `"MCP panel"`), `"the Skills panel"`, `"the Checkpoints panel"`, `"the Subagents panel"`, `"the Sessions panel"`, `"the Models panel"`, `"the Setup panel"`, `"the Settings panel"`.

**DOM byte-identity (review focus (a)).** `PanelScaffold`'s render is `<div id={panelTabpanelId(panel)} role="tabpanel" aria-labelledby={panelTabDomId(panel)} className="flex min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col"><ErrorBoundary region={region}>{children}</ErrorBoundary></div>` — the `id`/`role`/`aria-labelledby`/`className` are copied verbatim from all 9 original scaffolds (confirmed identical before extraction, see above), and each call site's `region` literal matches its original `ErrorBoundary region="…"` string byte-for-byte. `git diff -U0` of `App.tsx` shows exactly the 9 scaffold wrappers replaced by `<PanelScaffold>` calls (gate + inner `<XxxPanel/>` untouched) plus the `panelTabpanelId`/`panelTabDomId` imports removed from `App.tsx` (now unused there — every remaining reference is inside a doc comment, not code) and the new `PanelScaffold` import added; `ErrorBoundary`'s own import stays (still used by the untouched chat-panel wrapper).

**TDD (review focus (f)).** Wrote `webview/src/components/PanelScaffold.dom.test.tsx` FIRST — 3 tests: (1) the `tools` scaffold's `id`/`role="tabpanel"`/`aria-labelledby`/4-class `className` plus its children rendering; (2) a SECOND, DIFFERENT panel key (`mcp`, region `"the MCP panel"`) proving `id`/`aria-labelledby` are genuinely derived from the given key (not hardcoded) and differ from `tools`'s; (3) a thrown child inside `PanelScaffold` surfacing the `ErrorBoundary` fallback text `"Something went wrong in the MCP panel."`, proving `region` actually reaches the boundary (not just declared). Ran it against the not-yet-created component first — RED: `Failed to resolve import "./PanelScaffold"` (fails because the feature is missing, not a typo). Wrote the minimal `PanelScaffold.tsx` — GREEN: 3/3 passed. Rewired the 9 `App.tsx` scaffolds as callers; `npx vitest run webview/` — 89 files, 1828 passed (was 88/1825 pre-task — `ADR-025-AT`'s own after-count — 0 edits to `App.dom.test.tsx`, `PriorityTabs.dom.test.tsx`, or any other existing test file (confirmed by `git status`: only `App.tsx` modified, both `PanelScaffold.*` files new). **Filename note:** the brief's illustrative name was `PanelScaffold.test.tsx`; `vitest.config.ts`'s own header warns this codebase's 3 test projects are DISJOINT by filename suffix (`*.test.ts` / `*.dom.test.tsx` / `*.test-d.ts` only) and a file matching none of them "WILL NOT RUN and the suite will still be green" — a plain `.test.tsx` file would have been silently dead in every project. ## ADR-025-AV — 18 zero-dependency global actions extracted to `globalActions.ts` + the shaping helpers to `rpcShaped.ts`; residual-16 closed by evidence (task F1-4, WS-F1 — LAST task of WS-F1, FI-04, DOC-ONLY code-wise beyond the move)

**Outcome (task F1-4, WS-F1 — fourth and last task, 1 commit, BEHAVIOUR-PRESERVING PURE MOVE, NOT `[SEC]`/`[CONC]` per se but STRUCTURALLY ENFORCES the F-1 connection-global invariant):** re-grounded by symbol on `02ba769` (F1-3-closed HEAD, the live tip at kickoff) — every line anchor the brief cited (`onAddProviderKey` `:483` … `dispatchSetup` `:696`, the 3 helpers `:112-165`) matched exactly, zero drift from F1-3's own render-block move. FI-04 read: "`App()` is a ~1088-L god-component with ~35 inline RPC handlers across every domain." Measurement (re-confirmed here) found the crux: **18 of the handlers close over NOTHING component-scoped** — only module-level `bridge`, `requestShaped`/`requestShapedOptional`, `unwrapSetupResult`, and `is*` guards — and lived in `App()` by historical placement only. This task moves those 18 (verbatim) to a NEW `webview/src/globalActions.ts`, moves the 3 shaping helpers `requireShape`/`requestShaped`/`requestShapedOptional` (verbatim) to a NEW `webview/src/rpcShaped.ts` (required because both the 18 AND the staying checkpoint handlers depend on `requestShaped*`, and importing it back from `./App` would be circular), and closes the remaining 16 component-scoped handlers **by evidence** — the same evidentiary form `ADR-025-AN`'s FI-41 adjudicator accepted.

**1. The 18 moved — byte-identity, automated (review focus (a)).** Extracted the exact original line ranges (`:480-483`, `:535-543`, `:579-621`, `:623-642`, `:644-656`, `:658-671`, `:673-697`) from `git show 02ba769:webview/src/App.tsx`, mechanically de-indented by 2 spaces (the App()-body → module-top-level shift every moved declaration and its continuation lines undergo) and diffed byte-for-byte against `globalActions.ts`'s body (with only the `export ` prefix stripped for comparison): **134/134 lines identical, 0 diff.** No wording, literal, or expression was altered — including the comment prose (kept verbatim per the brief's explicit instruction, not touched even where a comment's cross-reference — e.g. addMcpServer's doc still says "the same `restoreCheckpoint`/`redoCheckpoint` idiom above" though those now live in a different file — reads as mild doc-drift; noted, not corrected, since only `rpcShaped.ts`'s dangling "below" cross-ref was in-scope for a wording fix).

**Closure-freedom re-confirmed for all 18** (brief's own re-verify instruction): `onAddProviderKey` (bridge only), `toggle` (bridge only), `reloadMcp` (bridge only), `addMcpServer`/`testMcpServer`/`authMcpServer`/`mcpCatalog`/`mcpCatalogInstall` (requestShaped + is* guard only, `mcpCatalogInstall`'s `wireParams` built from its own param only), `removeMcpServer`/`setMcpServerEnabled` (bridge only), `createSkill` (bridge only, `wireParams` from its own param only), `previewHubSkill`/`scanHubSkill`/`installHubSkill` (requestShaped + is* guard only), `uninstallHubSkill` (bridge only), `setConfig` (bridge only), `setNextEditToggle` (bridge only), `dispatchSetup` (bridge + `unwrapSetupResult` only) — **zero** reference `tab`/`state`/`dispatch`/`hostActions`/a `useState` setter; none needed to stay inline against the brief's prediction.

**2. The 3 helpers moved — byte-identity, automated (review focus (a)).** Same diff method against the original `:112-165` block (no de-indent needed — these were already module-level in App.tsx, so their own indentation carries over unchanged): **53/54 lines identical**; the one authorized difference is `requestShaped`'s own doc comment, whose "every ALWAYS-SHAPE correlated RPC call **below** repeated" no longer holds once the call sites live in a different file (`globalActions.ts`) — reworded to "call **site** repeated" (the brief's own explicit dangling-cross-ref-fix carve-out for this file only; the 18's comments got no such carve-out and were left untouched, see §1). `requireShape` stays non-exported (confirmed: its only 2 call sites are inside `requestShaped`/`requestShapedOptional`, both in the same file).

**3. `App.tsx` — render block 0-diff (review focus (d)), imports tsc-oracled (brief step 3).** `git diff 02ba769 -- webview/src/App.tsx | grep -c '^+'/'^-' ` → 23 insertions / 217 deletions, **all above the render block**; `sed -n '899,1348p'` of the pre-move file vs. the render block in the new file (`webview/src/App.tsx:705` onward) diffs **450/450 lines identical, 0-diff** — every JSX prop expression (`onAdd={addMcpServer}`, `toggle('toolsets.toggle', …)`, `dispatchSetup('setup.reconnectAgent', …)`, `onAddProviderKey={onAddProviderKey}`) is untouched because the import uses NAMED imports (`import { onAddProviderKey, toggle, reloadMcp, … } from './globalActions';`, never `import * as`). Unused-import removal was NOT guessed: `npm run check-types:all` (host + webview `tsc --noEmit`) was the oracle at every edit step and is 0-errors on the final state. Dropped from `App.tsx`'s `./protocol` type import: `ControlMethod`, `ControlRequestMethod`, `HubInstallResult`, `HubPreview`, `HubScan`, `McpAddParams`, `McpAddResult`, `McpCatalogData`, `McpCatalogInstallParams`, `McpCatalogInstallResult`, `McpTestResult`, `NextEditToggleSource`, `SetupMethod`, `SkillCreateParams` (13 types, all now imported by `globalActions.ts` instead); dropped from `./shapeGuards`: `asShape` (moved into `rpcShaped.ts`) and the 7 guards `isHubInstallResult`/`isHubPreview`/`isHubScan`/`isMcpAddResult`/`isMcpCatalogData`/`isMcpCatalogInstallResult`/`isMcpTestResult` (now in `globalActions.ts`; `isCheckpointRestoreResult` stays — the 3 checkpoint handlers still use it); dropped from `./state/panels`: `unwrapSetupResult` (now only `dispatchSetup`'s dependency, in `globalActions.ts`). `App.tsx` gained exactly 2 new imports: `requestShapedOptional` from `./rpcShaped` (the 3 staying checkpoint handlers) and the 18-name import from `./globalActions`.

**4. Wire + tagging invariant (review focus (b), adjudicator invariant 1).** Per-handler method/params/tag mapping, unchanged from the pre-move source: `onAddProviderKey`→`bridge.post({type:'model.addKey',slug})`; `toggle`→`toolsets.toggle`/`skills.toggle` (caller-supplied method, untagged); `reloadMcp`→`reload.mcp` untagged; `addMcpServer`→`mcp.add`, `testMcpServer`→`mcp.test`, `authMcpServer`→`mcp.auth`, `mcpCatalog`→`mcp.catalog`, `mcpCatalogInstall`→`mcp.catalogInstall` (all via `requestShaped`, tag omitted ⇒ `undefined`); `removeMcpServer`→`mcp.remove`, `setMcpServerEnabled`→`mcp.setEnabled` untagged; `createSkill`→`skills.create`, `previewHubSkill`→`skills.hubPreview`, `scanHubSkill`→`skills.hubScan`, `installHubSkill`→`skills.hubInstall`, `uninstallHubSkill`→`skills.hubUninstall` untagged; `setConfig`→`config.set` untagged; `setNextEditToggle`→`nextEdit.toggle` untagged (the exact F-1 regression site, now doubly guarded — see §5); `dispatchSetup`→caller-supplied `SetupMethod` untagged, routed through `unwrapSetupResult`. No method literal, params expression, or tag argument changed — proven both by the §1 line-diff and by `globalActions.test.ts`'s 18-row table (§7).

**5. F-1 now SCOPE-ENFORCED, not just documented (review focus (c), adjudicator invariant 2).** `grep -E "from 'react'|useState|useReducer|useCallback|TabState|\btab\." webview/src/globalActions.ts` → **3 hits, all inside doc-comment PROSE, 0 inside code**: the module header (`` `tab.tabId` tag cannot be written here `` — the brief's own required header wording, describing the guarantee), and 2 hits inherited verbatim from the pre-move `setConfig`/`setNextEditToggle` doc comments narrating the historical F-1 regression ("Tagging it with `tab.tabId` (the pre-fix bug)…", "A `tab.tabId` tag here would let an unrelated tab close…"). A comment-stripped re-check (`awk 'NR>38' … | grep -v '^\s*//' | grep -E "…\btab\."`) over the executable code alone: **0 hits**. This is the intended reading — the grep's job is to prove the CODE cannot construct a `tab.tabId` tag, and prose explaining why is not a violation of that; `globalActions.ts` imports no React, declares no `TabState`, and has no `tab` identifier in scope anywhere in its executable code, so a `tab.tabId` tag literally cannot be written here without first changing the module's function signatures — the invariant is now a compile-time/lexical fact, not a comment-only promise (the exact regression class the original F-1 finding was: `setConfig` wrongly `tab.tabId`-tagged, caught only by code review, not by any structural barrier).

**6. The residual 16 — closed by evidence (per the brief's Option B; review focus (e)):**

| handler | line (pre-move) | component-scoped dependency |
|---|---|---|
| `requestPanel` | `:516` | `tab` (scope-key resolution) + `dispatch` (via `fetchPanel`'s deps) |
| `restoreCheckpoint` | `:550` | `tab.rootId` (params) + `tab.tabId` (tag) |
| `redoCheckpoint` | `:567` | `tab.rootId` (params) + `tab.tabId` (tag) |
| `redoAllCheckpoint` | `:573` | `tab.rootId` (params) + `tab.tabId` (tag) |
| `openSetup` | `:700` | calls `selectPanel` (itself `dispatch`+`requestPanel`-scoped) |
| `loadMoreSessions` | `:717` | `tab.sessionId` (params) + `setSessionsLoadingMore`/`setSessionsLoadMoreError` (`useState` setters) |
| `loadSession` | `:738` | `setSessionLoadNotice` (setter) + `hostActions.loadSession` |
| `searchFiles` | `:749` | `tab.tabId` (tag) |
| `newSession` | `:754` | `dispatch` + `tab.tabId`/`tab.sessionId` |
| `selectPanel` | `:783` | `dispatch` + calls `requestPanel` |
| `openTab` | `:796` | `state.tabOrder` (guard) + `dispatch` |
| `selectTab` | `:809` | `state.tabs` (lookup) + `dispatch` |
| `closeTab` | `:824` | `state.tabs` (lookup) + `dispatch` + `bridge.rejectTab` (per-tab teardown) |
| `openDiff` | `:839` | `useCallback` dep `tab.sessionId` (a React hook — cannot exist outside a component) |
| `refreshErrorProp` | `:859` | `state.refreshError` + `dispatch` + calls `requestPanel` |
| `scopedRefreshErrorProp` | `:876` | `state` + `tab` (via `readScopedRefreshError`) + `dispatch` + calls `requestPanel` |

Every one of the 16 reads `tab`, `state`, `dispatch`, a `useState` setter, `hostActions`, or a React hook (`useCallback`) directly, or calls another handler that does (`openSetup`→`selectPanel`, `selectPanel`/`refreshErrorProp`/`scopedRefreshErrorProp`→`requestPanel`) — none can be moved to a module with no `tab`/`dispatch`/React in scope without either widening their signature to accept those as parameters (churn with no behaviour value, the same "cost of the other choice" `ADR-025-AN` weighed for `SetupCommandRouter`) or duplicating component state outside the component (a correctness hazard, not a cleanup). `withRefreshError` (`:896`) is a pure function of its already-computed argument — no component-scoped read — and was left in `App.tsx` per the brief's explicit "optional, not required" call; it neither reads nor writes a wire method, so it is not itself an RPC handler this finding's "~35 inline RPC handlers" counts.

**Decision — CLOSE FI-04 by (a) real extraction + (b) close-by-evidence, per the brief's Option B:** (a) the 18 truly zero-dependency handlers are now physically outside `App()`, in a module that cannot reach `tab` even if a future edit tried to add a tag — the exact regression class the original finding's F-1 fix brief existed for is now structurally foreclosed, not just documented; (b) the residual 16 are ALL genuinely component-scoped (table above — every dependency is a real `tab`/`state`/`dispatch`/setter/hook read, none a historical-placement accident), so no further extraction reduces `App()`'s responsibility count without either churn or a correctness hazard.

**7. TDD (review focus (f)) — source-lock repoint + mutate→RED→revert→GREEN.** `webview/src/rpc.test.ts:456-470` is a SOURCE LOCK that `readFileSync`s a webview file and regexes for the untagged `nextEdit.toggle` call; `setNextEditToggle` moving out of `App.tsx` requires repointing it. This is the ONLY existing-test edit in this task (confirmed: `git diff --stat` shows exactly this file + `App.tsx` + 4 new files touched). Ran the repointed lock — GREEN (30/30 in `rpc.test.ts`). **8. `globalActions.test.ts` — new, table derived from the pre-move source (review focus (g)).** 20 tests: 17 rows (`it.each`) covering every `bridge.request`-issuing export, asserting `toHaveBeenCalledWith(...expectedArgs)` — where `expectedArgs`' arity mirrors the ACTUAL call shape each site makes (a direct `bridge.request(method, params)` call records a 2-length `mock.calls` entry; a `requestShaped`-routed call always forwards a 3rd `tag` argument explicitly as `undefined`, a 3-length entry — verified empirically first that `toHaveBeenCalledWith` does NOT treat these as equal, so a single fixed arity would have silently mis-asserted half the rows) — PLUS a separate `mock.calls[0][2]` check on every row (the untagged proof, robust to either arity); 1 separate test for `onAddProviderKey` (`bridge.post`, not `.request`); 2 extra rows for `dispatchSetup`'s resolve/reject contract ({ok:false,reason}→rejects with the reason; {ok:true,…}→resolves with the result — `unwrapSetupResult` itself stays covered by `state/panels.ts`'s own tests, per the brief). Every expected `{method, params}` pair was read off `git show 02ba769:webview/src/App.tsx`, not invented. Discriminating power proven twice: (i) the mandatory `rpc.test.ts` mutation above; (ii) an additional spot-check — temporarily renamed `removeMcpServer`'s method literal to `'mcp.remove.WRONG'`, re-ran — 1 of 20 FAILED with a clean method-literal diff; reverted — 20/20 again. `rpcShaped.ts` also got a RECOMMENDED `rpcShaped.test.ts` (3 tests: bad-shape rejects with a method-named error; `requestShapedOptional` passes a bare `undefined` through unshaped; `tag` forwarded verbatim) — same discriminating-power spot-check performed (temporarily made `requestShapedOptional` always call `requireShape`, re-ran — 1 of 3 FAILED; reverted — 3/3 again). Both new test files needed the SAME `vi.stubGlobal('window', …)` + dynamic-`import()` idiom `bridge.test.ts` already established (this `webview-pure` vitest project has no jsdom; `bridge.ts`'s singleton construction touches `window`, and both `globalActions.ts`/`rpcShaped.ts` transitively import that singleton) — no new test infrastructure invented, the existing pattern reused.

**`App.dom.test.tsx` — 0-edit, green (review focus (h)).** `git diff --stat -- webview/src/App.dom.test.tsx` is empty. It `vi.spyOn(bridge, 'request'/'post')` on the REAL singleton and exercises `<App>` end-to-end, so the same spies intercept calls now routed through `globalActions.ts`/`rpcShaped.ts` without any change to the test itself. **Count discipline.** Baseline (`02ba769`, F1-3-closed HEAD, per `ADR-025-AU`'s own after-count) full `npm run gate`: **352 test files, 7669 passed | 15 skipped (7684), tsc 0 (host + webview).** Full-gate run after this task: **354 test files (+2: `globalActions.test.ts`, `rpcShaped.test.ts`), 7692 passed | 15 skipped (7707), tsc 0 (host + webview), no type errors.** Delta: **+2 test files, +23 tests** (20 in `globalActions.test.ts` + 3 in `rpcShaped.test.ts`), tsc 0. `src/rag/indexer.test.ts`'s known Windows ENOTEMPTY teardown flake appeared intermittently across several full-`vitest run` attempts (a different single test — or two — failing each time, always the same file, same `ENOTEMPTY` error, same temp-directory cleanup race under parallel workers) — every isolate-run (`npx vitest run src/rag/indexer.test.ts`) passed clean (59 passed | 2 skipped), and multiple full runs also passed clean end-to-end (354/354 files, 7692 passed | 15 skipped (7707)), confirming the flake is environmental and unrelated to this change (this task touches zero files under `src/`).

**Invariants re-measurable by the adjudicator (all TRUE, evidence above):**
1. Wire/tagging multiset identity across `{App.tsx, globalActions.ts, rpcShaped.ts}` vs. `02ba769:App.tsx` — §1/§4 (134/134-line diff + per-handler method table).
2. `globalActions.ts` has zero React/hooks/`TabState`/`tab.` reference IN CODE — §5 (3 comment-only hits documented and explained; 0 code hits, both greps shown).
3. Render block `App.tsx:899-1348`(pre)/`:705-1154`(post) 0-diff; `App.dom.test.tsx` 0-edit + green; `rpc.test.ts` edit limited to path + 2 titles — §3/§7/§8.
4. Host+webview `tsc` 0; vitest count rose (+23, not dropped); no `any`/`as`-cast/new `!`/`{...x,}` spread introduced — Count discipline + No-`any` sections above.
5. `App()` ≈ 954 L (down from 1089); 16 inline handlers, each listed with its component-scoped dependency — §6 table.

**★ FI-04 closed.** The 18 zero-dependency connection-global RPC handlers now live in `globalActions.ts`, a module that structurally cannot construct a `tab.tabId`-tagged request (no `tab` identifier, no React, in scope) — the F-1 invariant graduates from a documented convention to a lexical fact, closing off the exact regression class (`setConfig` wrongly tagged) the original F-1 finding fixed. The 3 shaping helpers live alongside their callers' shared dependency in `rpcShaped.ts`, avoiding a circular import between `App.tsx` and `globalActions.ts`. The residual 16 handlers are closed by evidence: every one is genuinely component-scoped (dependency table above), and no further split reduces responsibility count without churn or a correctness hazard — the same "no offsetting gain" reasoning `ADR-025-AN` applied to `SetupCommandRouter`. `App()` shrinks from ~1089 to 954 lines. **Last task of WS-F1** — the god-component workstream (F1-1 RPC-wrapper fold, F1-2 `RecoveryRow`, F1-3 `PanelScaffold`, F1-4 this extraction) is complete.
